1	# Build New Preview Workflow
2	
3	**Goal:** Turn user intent into a hardened, reviewable artifact.
4	
5	**CRITICAL:** If a step directs you to another snapshot file, read it fully and follow it. No exceptions.
6	
7	Subagents, when the capability is available, are an important part of this workflow. Use them as directed by the workflow steps.
8	If you need an explicit user instruction to run them, ask once now for the whole workflow run.
9	
10	## READY FOR DEVELOPMENT STANDARD
11	
12	A specification is "Ready for Development" when:
13	
14	- **Actionable**: Every task has a file path and specific action.
15	- **Logical**: Tasks ordered by dependency.
16	- **Testable**: All ACs use Given/When/Then.
17	- **Complete**: No placeholders or TBDs.
18	- **Sufficient**: No known requirement, acceptance, dependency, or implementation gaps remain unresolved.
19	- **Coherent**: No unresolved ambiguities or internal contradictions.
20	
21	## SCOPE STANDARD
22	
23	A specification should target a **single user-facing goal** within **900–1600 tokens**:
24	
25	- **Single goal**: One cohesive feature, even if it spans multiple layers/files. Multi-goal means >=2 **top-level independent shippable deliverables** — each could be reviewed, tested, and merged as a separate PR without breaking the others. Never count surface verbs, "and" conjunctions, or noun phrases. Never split cross-layer implementation details inside one user goal.
26	  - Split: "add dark mode toggle AND refactor auth to JWT AND build admin dashboard"
27	  - Don't split: "add validation and display errors" / "support drag-and-drop AND paste AND retry"
28	- **900–1600 tokens**: Optimal range for LLM consumption. Below 900 risks ambiguity; above 1600 risks context-rot in implementation agents.
29	- **Neither limit is a gate.** Both are proposals with user override.
30	
31	## Conventions
32	
33	- Every operational cross-file reference in this workflow is an absolute snapshot path. Open it directly; do not resolve it relative to a skill directory.
34	- `{project-root}`-prefixed paths resolve from the project working directory.
35	- Whenever this workflow captures or records a version-control revision, obtain the full canonical identifier directly from version control and preserve it verbatim.
36

57	## WORKFLOW ARCHITECTURE
58	
59	This uses **step-file architecture** for disciplined execution:
60	
61	- **Micro-file Design**: Each step is self-contained and followed exactly
62	- **Just-In-Time Loading**: Only load the current step file
63	- **Sequential Enforcement**: Complete steps in order, no skipping
64	- **State Tracking**: Persist progress via spec frontmatter and in-memory variables
65	- **Append-Only Building**: Build artifacts incrementally

69	1. **READ COMPLETELY**: Read the entire step file before acting

77	- **ALWAYS** read entire step file before execution
78	- **NEVER** skip steps or optimize the sequence

# Epic 1 Context: Find a Park

<!-- Compiled from planning artifacts. Edit freely. Regenerate with compile-epic-context if planning docs change. -->

## Goal

Deliver a small, keyless web app (a 2-hour take-home) that lets a resident find a local park in the NYC-area sample set on a map or in a list and open its details, on phone or desktop, by mouse, touch, or keyboard, with missing data handled honestly. The interviewer must be able to run it with one install and one start command and judge it from the README. A modest app that works and is well explained beats an ambitious one with broken core features; stop at 2 hours and document what is unfinished.

## Stories

- Story 1.1: Scaffold app and park data layer
- Story 1.2: Park list with keyboard selection
- Story 1.3: Park details with missing-data handling
- Story 1.4: Map of parks synced with selection
- Story 1.5: Responsive and accessibility polish with design applied
- Story 1.6: Tests and submission README

## Requirements & Constraints

- **Cross-cutting (every story):** no geolocation ever requested; no credentials or API keys; never invent missing data; `parks.sample.json` is never edited (copied into the app byte-for-byte); keyboard focus always visible.
- The list plus details view must be a complete alternative to the map. All 12 parks appear in the list, including ones with missing fields; parks without valid lat/lng get no marker but stay in the list and details.
- Every one of the 12 parks renders; null/missing/empty fields are omitted or labelled, never crash the UI. Failed images degrade without a broken-image icon.
- A data-load failure shows an error message with retry, never a blank screen.
- At 375px and desktop widths, list → details → back works with no horizontal scroll.
- Unredacted AI transcripts (Claude Code, exported by the developer before each fresh story context, and Claude Design) and design files are part of the submission; the README must say where they live.
- Non-goals: backend, accounts, deployment, data editing. Search, filters, current location only if core is done with time to spare.
- Where the design and the spec disagree, the spec wins.

## Technical Decisions

- **Stack:** React + Vite, Tailwind CSS (mobile-first), `react-leaflet` + Leaflet with OpenStreetMap standard tiles (keyless, attribution visible), Vitest + React Testing Library. No component library.
- **Data loading (readiness decision):** the spec says static import, but loading/error/retry states require a mechanism that can actually fail. Use one that can (e.g. `fetch` from `public/` or a dynamic `import()`) and document the choice in the README.
- **Routing (readiness decision):** React Router. `/` is the list, `/parks/:id` is details (supersedes the design's `?park=` option). Unknown id shows "park not found" with a way back. Browser Back must work.
- **Data shape:** array of park objects with `id`, `name` (treat as required); `description`, `location.{lat,lng,address}`, `amenities` (kebab-case tokens), `hours` (free text, shown as written), `images` (URL array), `acreage`, `rating` (0–5) may each be missing, null, or empty. Read nested fields null-safely (`park.location?.address`).
- **Normalization:** one null-safe module applies safe defaults with no fabricated values, sorts parks A–Z and numbers them 1–N; that number is shared by list cards and map markers. Centralize fallback rules in a small helper (e.g. `display(value, fallback)`).
- **Image URL transformation:** pixel art moves from `design/park-images/` to `src/assets/park-images/`. At load time each image URL maps by filename stem to the bundled asset (`.../prospect-1.jpg` → `park-images/prospect-1.png`); unmatched URLs pass through unchanged so `onError` fallback still applies. Do not mutate source data. Document in README.
- **Amenity labels:** replace `-` with a space and capitalize the first letter (`dog-run` → "Dog run"); unknown tokens are humanized, never dropped.
- **Map:** initial view fits bounds of parks with coordinates. Leaflet default marker icons need explicit asset imports under Vite (or use `L.divIcon` with a `<button>` inside). Markers are focusable, named "N. Park name"; the selected marker is amber and larger.
- **Tailwind tokens:** paper `#F6F4EE`, surface `#FFFFFF`, line `#DAD6CB`, ink `#1B2620`, muted `#4F5B54`, forest `#1E5B3F` / dark `#123826`, tag bg `#E9EFE7` / fg `#23472F`, amber `#B45309`, missing bg `#F0EDE5` / line `#C9C4B6`. Font: Public Sans (Google Fonts, 400–800). Focus ring: `focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-[3px] focus-visible:outline-amber focus-visible:outline-offset-2`.
- **Tests must cover** every known data gap (see UX section), a failing image, an unmatched image URL, the URL transformation without mutation, and the keyboard list → details → back flow.

## UX & Interaction Patterns

- **Landmarks:** `<header>`, `<main>`, `<aside aria-label="Parks">`, map region labelled "Map of parks". One `<h1>` per view; no clickable `<div>`s; icon-only buttons have `aria-label`s; touch targets ≥ 44×44px; text contrast ≥ 4.5:1.
- **Desktop (≥ 768px):** green header (app name "Find a Park", subtitle, park count); ~400px independently scrolling sidebar; map fills the rest. Selecting a park switches the sidebar to details, starting with an "All parks" back control.
- **Phone (< 768px):** single column with a List / Map toggle of two `<button>`s using `aria-pressed`, List default. Tapping a marker shows a preview card ("Park N of 12", name, address, hours, "View park details", close button "Close park preview"). Details take the full screen with an "All parks" back link.
- **Focus management:** opening details moves focus to the details heading; returning to the list moves focus to the originating card.
- **Park card:** single `<button>`/link per card (one Tab stop) in a `<ul>`; number badge, name (17px bold), address, first three amenities as tags plus "+N more".
- **Details order:** back control, photo area ("Photo 1 of N", alt text naming the park), name as `<h1>` (26–28px, 800), address, "Open in OpenStreetMap" link from lat/lng, `<dl>` fact tiles (Hours, Size in acres, Rating "4.7 / 5"), About, all amenities as checked tags.
- **Missing-data copy:** address → "Address not listed" (muted); description null → "No description has been provided for this park."; rating null → "Not yet rated" (never 0/empty stars); empty or failed images → dashed box "No photos for this park yet"; hours/acreage → "Not listed"; empty amenities → hide section or "No amenities listed"; no lat/lng → no marker, no OSM link. Missing fact tiles use the muted style (missing bg, dashed border, italic), as on the phone screen; the desktop mock's normal-style "Not yet rated" tile is a mistake.
- **Sample gaps:** Old Mill Botanical Garden (null description), Cedar Hill Nature Preserve (empty images, null rating; coordinates look like a placeholder, note in Known issues), East Ridge Trailhead (empty images), Highland Dog Park (missing address).
- **Loading/error states:** "Loading parks…" and "Couldn't load parks. Try again." with a retry button, using the card style.
- Visual reference: `design/screens/` (1280×800 and 390×844). `design/source/*.dc.html` is reference only, not app code.

## Cross-Story Dependencies

- 1.1 provides the data layer, routing, layout shell, tokens, and bundled images that all later stories build on.
- 1.2 needs the 1.3 back control for its focus-return-to-card criterion. **Readiness decision:** in 1.2, verify focus return using browser Back; 1.3 confirms it via the "All parks" control.
- 1.4 relies on the shared A–Z numbering from 1.1 and the selected-park route to highlight markers and open details.
- 1.5 is the full responsive/accessibility pass over 1.2–1.4 (which ship baseline accessibility).
- 1.6 tests cover behaviour from 1.1–1.3; the README records decisions from all stories, including the data-load mechanism and React Router choice.

| CAP-4 Accessible, responsive UI | 1.2, 1.3, 1.4 (baseline), 1.5 (full pass) |
| CAP-5 README | 1.6 |
### Story 1.5: Responsive and accessibility polish with design applied

As a resident on a phone or using a keyboard or screen reader,
I want the app to match the designed layouts and work well in every mode,
So that the main tasks are easy everywhere.

**Acceptance Criteria:**

**Given** the screens in `design/screens/`
**When** the app is compared against them at 1280×800 and 390×844
**Then** the layout, tokens, type sizes, corner radius, and spacing match `design/README.md`, except where the SPEC overrides the design

**Given** desktop width (≥ 768px)
**When** the app renders
**Then** a green header shows the app name, subtitle, and park count, with a ~400px independently scrolling sidebar and the map filling the rest

**Given** phone width (< 768px)
**When** the app renders
**Then** a single column shows a List / Map toggle of two `<button>`s with `aria-pressed`, List by default
**And** details take the full screen with an "All parks" back link in the header

**Given** widths of 375px and 1280px
**When** the user completes list → details → back
**Then** there is no horizontal scroll and every interactive element shows the amber focus-visible ring

**Given** the whole app
**When** it is checked for accessibility
**Then** there are no clickable `<div>`s, there is one `<h1>` per view, every icon-only button has an `aria-label`, touch targets are at least 44×44px, and text contrast is at least 4.5:1

## Intent

**Problem:** `/parks/:id` shows only the park name (`ParkDetailsPlaceholder`). Residents can't see hours, size, rating, description, photos or amenities, or tell when a value is missing rather than wrong (CAP-3).

**Approach:** Replace the placeholder with a `ParkDetails` view in this order: an "All parks" back link, the photo area, the name as `<h1>`, the address, an "Open in OpenStreetMap" link, a `<dl>` of Hours/Size/Rating tiles, About, and Amenities. Every missing value gets the labelled fallback from `design/README.md`. Focus moves to the `<h1>` when details open.

## Boundaries & Constraints

**Always:** render the normalized park exactly as given and never invent a value; fallback copy matches the design table word for word; missing-value tiles use `bg-missing-bg`, a dashed `border-missing-line` and italic text; `focus-ring` and ≥ 44px targets on the back link and the OSM link; reuse `amenityLabel` from `src/data/format.js`; keep the `NotFound` branch and the `onOpen` id recording.

**Never:** a photo gallery or carousel (first photo only); map, markers or marker highlight (1.4); the desktop sidebar/phone header layout split or the List/Map toggle (1.5); an "Open now" badge or hours parsing; star icons; clickable `<div>`s; editing `parks.sample.json` or the normalizer.

**Decisions (agent-made, user-invisible or design-permitted):** empty amenities shows the Amenities heading with "No amenities listed" rather than hiding it. Rating shows one decimal (`4` → "4.0 / 5"). Size shows "N acres" ("1 acre" when N is 1). Photo alt is "Photo of {name}". The back control is a `<Link to="/">`, so the existing `lastParkId` mechanism returns focus to the card.

## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix

| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Output / Behavior | Error Handling |
|----------|--------------|---------------------------|----------------|
| Full data | Prospect Park | all sections; "Photo 1 of 2"; tiles "6:00 AM - 1:00 AM", "526 acres", "4.7 / 5"; 7 checked amenity tags | N/A |
| Missing address | Highland Dog Park | "Address not listed" in `text-muted` | N/A |
| Null description | Old Mill Botanical Garden | About reads "No description has been provided for this park." | N/A |
| Null rating | Cedar Hill | Rating tile "Not yet rated", muted style; no "0" | N/A |
| No images | Cedar Hill, East Ridge | dashed "No photos for this park yet" box, no `<img>` | N/A |
| Image fails | `<img>` fires `error` | the no-photo box replaces it | no broken-image icon |
| Missing hours / acreage | synthetic park | the tile reads "Not listed", muted style | N/A |
| Empty amenities | synthetic `amenities: []` | "No amenities listed" | N/A |
| No coordinates | synthetic `hasCoords: false` | no OSM link | N/A |
| Unknown amenity | `wifi`, `dog-run` | "Wifi", "Dog run" | humanized, never dropped |
| Back control | click "All parks" | `/` renders; that park's card has focus | N/A |

</frozen-after-approval>

## Code Map

- `src/App.jsx` -- `ParkDetailsPlaceholder` (find park by `:id`, call `onOpen`, `NotFound` fallback) is the code to replace. Move that lookup into the new component, or wrap it. `linkClass`/`cardClass` are reusable styles. `lastParkId` + `ParkList focusParkId` already restore card focus on any return to `/`.
- `src/data/parks.js` -- normalized park `{ id, name, number, description, address, lat, lng, hasCoords, amenities, hours, images, acreage, rating }`. Missing values are `null`/`[]`, `rating` is in 0–5 or `null`, and `images` already resolve to bundled assets. Don't change it.
- `src/data/format.js` -- `amenityLabel`; add the small display helpers here (the epic asks for a central `display(value, fallback)`-style rule set).
- `src/index.css` -- tokens `missing-bg`, `missing-line`, `tag-bg`, `tag-fg`, `muted`, `forest`, `line`, `surface`; the `focus-ring` utility.
- `src/App.test.jsx` -- the `renderAt(path)`, `okResponse()` (structuredClone of the sample, so tests can mutate it for synthetic parks) and `TestNav` patterns.
- `design/screens/02-desktop-park-selected.png`, `06-phone-details.png`, `07-phone-missing-data.png` -- the visuals: bordered "‹ All parks" button, a rounded photo with a dark "Photo 1 of N" pill at bottom-right, a 3-column tile grid, ✓ tags.
- OSM URL: `https://www.openstreetmap.org/?mlat={lat}&mlon={lng}#map=16/{lat}/{lng}`.

## Tasks & Acceptance

**Execution:**
- [x] `src/data/format.js` -- add `formatRating(n)` → "4.7 / 5" or `null`, `formatAcres(n)` → "526 acres" / "1 acre" or `null`, `osmUrl(lat, lng)`, and `display(value, fallback)` → `{ text, missing }` so the view applies every fallback through one rule.
- [x] `src/components/ParkDetails.jsx` -- `ParkDetails({ park })` renders the sections in order: `PhotoArea` (keyed by park id, `onError` → no-photo box), an `<h1 tabIndex={-1}>` focused on mount, a `<dl>` of `FactTile`s (muted variant when the value is missing), About, and Amenities as a `<ul>` of ✓ tags.
- [x] `src/App.jsx` -- the `/parks/:id` route renders `ParkDetails` for a found park (keep `onOpen` and `NotFound`); delete `ParkDetailsPlaceholder`.
- [x] `src/data/format.test.js`, `src/App.test.jsx` -- unit-test the new helpers; test every matrix row (synthetic rows by editing the cloned sample in a custom fetch mock), section order, and focus on the heading after opening from the list.

**Acceptance Criteria:**
- Given the list, when the user opens a park by keyboard, then the details `<h1>` has focus and is the only `<h1>` in the view.
- Given details, when the user activates "All parks", then the list renders and focus is on that park's card.
- Given a park with images, then one `<img>` shows with alt naming the park and a "Photo 1 of N" label.
- Given any of the 12 sample parks, when details render, then nothing crashes and no value reads "null", "undefined" or "NaN".

Code review 2026-10-14 (blind-hunter, edge-case-hunter, verification-gap, acceptance-auditor) of commit `c08a597`.

- [x] [Review][Patch] The OSM link's "(opens in new tab)" hint and its `target`/`rel` are untested: the name regex `/^Open in OpenStreetMap/` is a prefix match, so the BH6 fix can be deleted without any test failing [src/App.test.jsx:375]

**Rejected**

- `false` — Spec `status: 'done'` vs sprint-status `review` (BH, AA): the fix would edit the spec under review, and this review's status sync settles it.
- `false` — Sprint status leaves 1-2 `in-progress` (BH): this was set deliberately by `6430967` after 1-2's review left patches open. This change did not cause it.
- `false` — Implementation Notes / Spec Change Log are empty (BH): the fix would edit the spec under review. The applied patches show in the diff and the triage log.
- `false` — `osmUrl` could emit `NaN` in the href (BH, EC, AA): the normalizer sets `hasCoords` only when `lat`/`lng` pass `numberInRange`, so the link never renders with non-finite coordinates.
- `low` — Null `name` gives an empty `<h1>` and alt "Photo of null", which the no-null test misses (EC, BH, AA): all 12 sample parks have names, `name` is required by the data contract, and the fix adds a fallback branch. This is the same call as 1.1 EC2, 1.2 BH1 and 1.3 pass-1 EC1/EC4.
- `low` — `0` acres / `0.0 / 5` rendered as real values (BH): the normalizer treats 0 as valid, no sample park has 0, and the design's "do not show 0" applies only to `null`.
- `false` — No range check on rating (BH): `normalizePark` already nulls ratings outside 0–5.
- `false` — Every address is muted, so the missing-address test is weak (BH): the design table asks only for "secondary text colour", and the design shows every address muted. This is the same call as pass-1 BH2.
- `false` — Deep link to `/parks/:id` moves focus to the `<h1>` (BH): focusing the page heading on route load is the intended SPA pattern, so nothing bad happens.
- `false` — The deferred-work entry lacks an id/status (BH): the ledger is new, and the loop sweep's `--migrate` handles pre-DW-format entries.
- `false` — "Photo 1 of N" implies a gallery (BH): `design/README.md` asks for exactly this label on a single image.
- `low` — A dash-only amenity token (`"-"`) renders an empty tag (EC): no sample data does this, and the fix adds a filter.
- `false` — Same park id re-rendered with new images keeps a stale failed state (EC): parks load once, and no path changes a park's images in place.
- `low` — `formatAcres` has no rounding or thousands separator (EC): sample acreages are integers ≤ 640. This is the same call as pass-1 EC3/BH4.
- `low` — `toFixed(1)` rounds a two-decimal rating (AA): no sample rating has two decimals, and one decimal is a spec Decision.
- `false` — Park-to-park refocus and `PhotoArea` reset are untested (AA, VG other): this is already deferred in `deferred-work.md` (VG2), so it is not a new finding.

## Design Notes

Focus-to-heading uses a ref plus `useEffect` keyed on `park.id`, so moving from one park to another also refocuses. The heading needs `tabIndex={-1}` to be focusable from code, and it gets no `focus-ring`, since a programmatic focus target is not a Tab stop. `PhotoArea` keeps a `failed` state and is keyed by park id, so a failure on one park does not hide another park's photo.

Missing-value tile golden example:

```jsx
<div className={missing ? 'border-dashed border-missing-line bg-missing-bg' : 'border-line bg-surface'}>
  <dt className="text-sm text-muted">Rating</dt>
  <dd className={missing ? 'italic text-muted' : 'font-semibold'}>{value ?? fallback}</dd>
</div>
```

  evidence: Removing `key={park.id}` from `PhotoArea` or changing the heading-focus effect deps to `[]` passes all tests. Unreachable until Story 1.4 adds map selection that switches parks without passing through `/`.

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, Route, Routes, useParams } from 'react-router'
import ParkDetails from './components/ParkDetails.jsx'
import ParkList from './components/ParkList.jsx'
import { useParks } from './data/useParks.js'

const cardClass = 'rounded-[10px] border border-line bg-surface p-4'
const linkClass = 'focus-ring rounded text-forest underline'

function NotFound() {
  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-lg font-bold">Park not found</h1>
      <p className="mt-2">
        <Link to="/" className={linkClass}>
          Back to all parks
        </Link>
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

function ParkDetailsRoute({ parks, onOpen }) {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const park = parks.find((p) => p.id === id)

  // Record the opened park so the list can return focus to its card.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (park) onOpen(park.id)
  }, [park, onOpen])

  if (!park) return <NotFound />

  return <ParkDetails park={park} />
}

function ParksPanel({ status, parks, retry, lastParkId, setLastParkId }) {
  const clearLastParkId = useCallback(() => setLastParkId(null), [setLastParkId])

  if (status === 'loading') {
    return (
      <p role="status" className={cardClass}>
        Loading parks…
      </p>
    )
  }

  if (status === 'error') {
    return (
      <div role="alert" className={cardClass}>
        <p>Couldn't load parks. Try again.</p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={retry}
          className="focus-ring mt-3 min-h-11 rounded-lg bg-forest px-4 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-forest-dark"
        >
          Retry
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route
        path="/"
        element={
          <ParkList parks={parks} focusParkId={lastParkId} onFocusUsed={clearLastParkId} />
        }
      />
      <Route
        path="/parks/:id"
        element={<ParkDetailsRoute parks={parks} onOpen={setLastParkId} />}
      />
      <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
    </Routes>
  )
}

export default function App() {
  const { status, parks, retry } = useParks()
  // Id of the last-opened park. Lives in App state (not location.state) so
  // focus can return to its card after browser Back, which carries no state.
  const [lastParkId, setLastParkId] = useState(null)

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <header className="bg-forest px-5 py-4 text-white">
        <p className="text-xl font-extrabold">Find a Park</p>
        <p className="text-sm">New York City area</p>
        {status === 'ready' && <p className="text-sm">{parks.length} parks</p>}
      </header>
      <main className="flex flex-1 flex-col">
        <aside aria-label="Parks" className="p-5">
          <ParksPanel
            status={status}
            parks={parks}
            retry={retry}
            lastParkId={lastParkId}
            setLastParkId={setLastParkId}
          />
        </aside>
        <section aria-label="Map of parks" className="flex-1 p-5">
          <p className="text-muted">Map coming soon.</p>
        </section>
      </main>
    </div>
  )
}
@import "tailwindcss";

/* Design tokens from design/README.md */
@theme {
  --color-paper: #F6F4EE;
  --color-surface: #FFFFFF;
  --color-line: #DAD6CB;
  --color-ink: #1B2620;
  --color-muted: #4F5B54;
  --color-forest: #1E5B3F;
  --color-forest-dark: #123826;
  --color-tag-bg: #E9EFE7;
  --color-tag-fg: #23472F;
  --color-amber: #B45309;
  --color-missing-bg: #F0EDE5;
  --color-missing-line: #C9C4B6;

  --font-sans: "Public Sans", "Segoe UI", system-ui, sans-serif;
}

/* Amber focus ring: apply `focus-ring` to every interactive element. */
@utility focus-ring {
  &:focus-visible {
    outline-style: solid;
    outline-width: 3px;
    outline-color: var(--color-amber);
    outline-offset: 2px;
  }
}

body {
  @apply bg-paper text-ink font-sans;
}

# Find a Park: design handoff

UI designs for the Find a Park take-home. They were drawn on a Claude design canvas before any code was written, and they are the visual spec for the React + Tailwind + Leaflet build. The full AI transcript of the design session is in the repo alongside this folder.

**These are reference designs, not code.** Build with React, Tailwind and react-leaflet (no component library), and use the screenshots plus the rules below. Where the brief and these designs disagree, the brief wins.

## Contents

| File | What it shows |
|---|---|
| `screens/01-desktop-list-map.png` | Desktop default: park list on the left, map on the right |
| `screens/02-desktop-park-selected.png` | Desktop with a park selected: details replace the list, the marker is highlighted |
| `screens/03-desktop-missing-data.png` | Desktop details for a park with missing data (Cedar Hill: no photos, no rating) |
| `screens/04-phone-list.png` | Phone, List tab |
| `screens/05-phone-map-selected.png` | Phone, Map tab, with a park selected (preview card) |
| `screens/06-phone-details.png` | Phone details, full data (Prospect Park) |
| `screens/07-phone-missing-data.png` | Phone details, missing data (Cedar Hill) |
| `source/*.dc.html` | Canvas source files, for reference only. They need the canvas runtime and are **not** meant to run in the app. |

Screenshots are at 2x: desktop at 1280×800, phone at 390×844. The map in every screenshot is a drawn stand-in. The real app uses Leaflet with OpenStreetMap tiles.

## Layout and behaviour

### Desktop (≥ 768px, Tailwind `md:`)

- A green header with the app name, a subtitle and the park count.
- Two columns:
  - **Left sidebar (~400px):** scrolls on its own.
  - **Map:** fills the rest of the screen.
- **Sidebar, list mode:** an "All parks" heading, a one-line hint, then one card per park.
- **Selecting a park** from either the list or the map:
  - The sidebar switches to details mode, starting with an "All parks" back button.
  - That park's marker turns amber and gets bigger.
  - The URL should change too (e.g. `?park=prospect-park` or `/parks/:id`), so details can be linked and the browser Back button works.
- **Going back to the list:** move focus to the card of the park you came from.
- **Opening details:** move focus to the details heading, so keyboard and screen-reader users land in the right place.

### Phone (< 768px)

- One column. A **List / Map** toggle sits under the header, made of two real `<button>`s with `aria-pressed`. List is the default.
- **Tapping a marker** opens a preview card at the bottom of the map. It shows the park's position in the list ("Park 8 of 12"), its name, address and hours, a **View park details** button and a close button.
- **Details** take the whole screen, with an "All parks" back link in the header.

### List ↔ map link

- Parks are sorted A–Z and numbered 1–12. The same number appears on the list card and on the map marker, so the two views always match.
- Every marker is focusable and named "8. Prospect Park" for screen readers. Use `L.divIcon` with a `<button>` inside it, or set `keyboard: true` and `alt`/`title` on the markers.
- The list is the main way to choose a park. The map is never the only way.

## Park card (list)

- Number badge, park name (17px bold), address, and the first three amenities as tags. If there are more, add a "+N more" tag.
- The whole card is a single `<button>` (or a link once there is routing), so it gets keyboard focus and one Tab stop.

## Details

The details view shows, from top to bottom:

1. Photo area (see the missing-data rules below)
2. Park name as the page heading (26–28px, 800 weight), then the address
3. An **Open in OpenStreetMap** link built from the park's lat/lng (`https://www.openstreetmap.org/?mlat={lat}&mlon={lng}#map=16/{lat}/{lng}`). This was added during design and is easy to drop if you don't want it.
4. Three fact tiles: **Hours**, **Size** (acres), **Rating** ("4.7 / 5")
5. **About** (the description)
6. **Amenities**: all of them, as tags with a check mark. Turn the raw values into labels by replacing `-` with a space and capitalising the first letter (`dog-run` → "Dog run").

## Missing data: never invent a value

The brief requires this, and the sample data has every one of these cases.

| Field | When missing / null / empty | Show |
|---|---|---|
| `location.address` | Highland Dog Park | "Address not listed" in the secondary text colour |
| `description` | Old Mill Botanical Garden (`null`) | "No description has been provided for this park." |
| `rating` | Cedar Hill (`null`) | "Not yet rated" (do not show 0 or blank stars) |
| `images` | Cedar Hill, East Ridge (`[]`) | A dashed box: image icon + "No photos for this park yet" |
| `images` failing to load | **Every** sample URL (`images.example.com` doesn't exist) | The same fallback, triggered by `onError` on `<img>`; never a broken-image icon |
| `hours` | not in the sample, but handle it | "Not listed" |
| `acreage` | not in the sample, but handle it | "Not listed" |
| `amenities` | not in the sample, but handle it | Hide the Amenities section, or show "No amenities listed" |
| `location.lat/lng` | not in the sample, but handle it | Keep the park in the list, leave it off the map, and hide the map link |

Fact tiles with a missing value use the muted look from screen 07: `#F0EDE5` background, dashed `#C9C4B6` border, italic text. *Remark: the desktop screenshot (03) shows "Not yet rated" with the normal tile style. That is a mistake in the mock, and the phone version is the intended look.*

Wrap every field in a small helper (e.g. `display(value, fallback)`) so the rules stay in one place. Never let a missing nested field crash the app (`park.location?.address`).

## Design tokens (Tailwind)

Add these under `theme.extend` (or as CSS variables with Tailwind v4 `@theme`):

```js
colors: {
  paper:   '#F6F4EE', // page background
  surface: '#FFFFFF', // cards, tiles
  line:    '#DAD6CB', // card borders
  ink:     '#1B2620', // main text
  muted:   '#4F5B54', // secondary text (≈7:1 on white)
  forest:  { DEFAULT: '#1E5B3F', dark: '#123826' }, // header, primary buttons, markers, links
  tag:     { bg: '#E9EFE7', fg: '#23472F' },         // amenity tags
  amber:   '#B45309', // selected marker and focus ring
  missing: { bg: '#F0EDE5', line: '#C9C4B6' },       // missing-value tiles, no-photo box
  map:     { land: '#E4E8DF', water: '#B9D3DC' },    // only for the drawn map; real tiles replace these
},
fontFamily: { sans: ['"Public Sans"', '"Segoe UI"', 'system-ui', 'sans-serif'] },
```

- **Font:** Public Sans from Google Fonts, weights 400/500/600/700/800.
- **Type sizes:**
  - Body 15–16px; card titles 17px bold
  - Details heading 26–28px (800 weight); section headings 17–18px
  - Small labels 13px; nothing smaller except the map attribution
- **Corner radius:** cards 10px, buttons 8–10px, tags fully rounded.
- **Spacing:** sidebar padding 20px (16px on phone), 10px between cards, 14–16px inside cards.

## Accessibility

These checks match what the brief evaluates:

- **Focus ring:** every interactive element gets a visible ring, `focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-[3px] focus-visible:outline-amber focus-visible:outline-offset-2`. Never remove outlines without putting this ring in their place.
- **Touch targets:** at least 44×44px, including the zoom buttons, the close button and the back button.
- **Real elements:** use `<button>`, `<a href>`, `<ul>/<li>` for the list, `<dl>/<dt>/<dd>` for the fact tiles, and one `<h1>` per view. No clickable `<div>`s.
- **Landmarks:** `<header>`, `<main>`, an `<aside aria-label="Parks">` for the sidebar, and a map section labelled "Map of parks".
- **Labels:** icon-only buttons need an `aria-label` ("Zoom in", "Close park preview").
- **Contrast:** text has at least 4.5:1 contrast. The colours above meet that, including white on forest.
- **Location:** never ask for the user's location; the app must work without it. Centre the map on a fixed view, or fit it to the parks' bounds.

## Remarks and open decisions

- **Municipality:** the parks span Brooklyn, Manhattan, Queens and the Bronx and are mostly fictional (Prospect Park is real). The designs call the area "New York City area". Say so in the project README.
- **Cedar Hill coordinates** (40.7128, -74.006) are exactly the standard "New York City" point, so they are probably a placeholder. The park is shown as given; mention it in Known issues.
- **Short addresses** such as "Ridge Rd" and "Shore Pkwy" are shown as given, without adding a city.
- **Not designed:**
  - Search and filters (optional in the brief)
  - Current-location support (optional)
  - A loading state and an error state for when `parks.json` fails to load. Both are worth adding: a short "Loading parks…" message and a "Couldn't load parks. Try again." message with a retry button, using the same card style.
- **Hours** are shown exactly as written in the data ("Dawn to dusk", "24 hours"). An "Open now" badge was left out on purpose, because parsing these strings reliably is out of scope.
- **Photos:** there is no gallery. The mock shows the first photo with a "Photo 1 of N" label. Showing a single image, or a simple row of thumbnails, is fine.
- **Map tiles:** OpenStreetMap's tile server needs no API key; keep its attribution visible. For real public use, switch to a tile provider that allows production traffic. That belongs in the README's next steps.
import { Fragment, useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import { amenityLabel } from '../data/format.js'

const MAX_TAGS = 3

function ParkCard({ park, linkRef }) {
  const shown = park.amenities.slice(0, MAX_TAGS)
  const extra = park.amenities.length - shown.length
  const tags = shown.map(amenityLabel)
  if (extra > 0) tags.push(`+${extra} more`)

  return (
    <li>
      <Link
        ref={linkRef}
        to={`/parks/${park.id}`}
        className="focus-ring flex min-h-11 items-start gap-3 rounded-[10px] border border-line bg-surface p-4 hover:border-forest"
      >
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-forest text-sm font-bold text-white"
        >
          {park.number}
        </span>
        <span className="sr-only">{park.number}.</span>{' '}
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <span className="block text-[17px] font-bold">{park.name}</span>{' '}
          {park.address ? (
            <span className="block text-muted">{park.address}</span>
          ) : (
            <span className="block text-muted">Address not listed</span>
          )}{' '}
          {tags.length > 0 && (
            <span data-testid="park-tags" className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {tags.map((label, index) => (
                <Fragment key={`${index}-${label}`}>
                  <span className="rounded-full bg-tag-bg px-2.5 py-0.5 text-sm text-tag-fg">
                    {label}
                  </span>{' '}
                </Fragment>
              ))}
            </span>
          )}
        </span>
        <svg
          aria-hidden="true"
          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          className="size-5 shrink-0 self-center text-muted"
          fill="none"
          stroke="currentColor"
          strokeWidth="2"
        >
          <path d="m9 6 6 6-6 6" />
        </svg>
      </Link>
    </li>
  )
}

/**
 * The "All parks" list. Parks are rendered in loader order with loader numbers.
 * If `focusParkId` matches a card on mount, that card's link gets focus and
 * `onFocusUsed` is called so a later fresh visit does not steal focus.
 */
export default function ParkList({ parks, focusParkId, onFocusUsed }) {
  const linkRefs = useRef(new Map())

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!focusParkId) return
    linkRefs.current.get(focusParkId)?.focus()
    onFocusUsed?.()
    // Run once on mount: the id is read once, then cleared by the parent.
  }, [])

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-lg font-bold">All parks</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 text-muted">Choose a park to see hours, amenities and directions.</p>
      <ul className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3">
        {parks.map((park) => (
          <ParkCard
            key={park.id}
            park={park}
            linkRef={(node) => {
              if (node) linkRefs.current.set(park.id, node)
              else linkRefs.current.delete(park.id)
            }}
          />
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  )
}
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import { amenityLabel, display, formatAcres, formatRating, osmUrl } from '../data/format.js'

// Fallback copy, word for word from design/README.md.
const NO_ADDRESS = 'Address not listed'
const NO_DESCRIPTION = 'No description has been provided for this park.'
const NO_RATING = 'Not yet rated'
const NO_PHOTOS = 'No photos for this park yet'
const NOT_LISTED = 'Not listed'
const NO_AMENITIES = 'No amenities listed'

function NoPhoto() {
  return (
    <div
      className="flex aspect-[16/9] flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-[10px] border-2 border-dashed border-missing-line bg-missing-bg p-4 text-center text-muted"
    >
      <svg
        aria-hidden="true"
        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
        className="size-7"
        fill="none"
        stroke="currentColor"
        strokeWidth="1.75"
        strokeLinecap="round"
        strokeLinejoin="round"
      >
        <rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="16" rx="2" />
        <path d="m3 16 5-5 4 4 3-3 6 6" />
        <circle cx="15.5" cy="8.5" r="1.5" />
      </svg>
      <p>{NO_PHOTOS}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

/** First photo only, with a "Photo 1 of N" label. A load failure swaps in the no-photo box. */
function PhotoArea({ park }) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false)
  const src = park.images[0]

  if (!src || failed) return <NoPhoto />

  return (
    <figure className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-[10px] bg-missing-bg">
      <img
        src={src}
        alt={`Photo of ${park.name}`}
        onError={() => setFailed(true)}
        className="block aspect-[16/9] w-full object-cover"
      />
      <figcaption className="absolute right-2 bottom-2 rounded-full bg-ink/85 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[13px] font-semibold text-white">
        Photo 1 of {park.images.length}
      </figcaption>
    </figure>
  )
}

function FactTile({ label, value }) {
  const { missing, text } = value
  return (
    <div
      className={`min-w-0 break-words rounded-[10px] border p-3 ${
        missing ? 'border-dashed border-missing-line bg-missing-bg' : 'border-line bg-surface'
      }`}
    >
      <dt className="text-sm text-muted">{label}</dt>
      <dd className={missing ? 'italic text-muted' : 'font-semibold'}>{text}</dd>
    </div>
  )
}

/** Full details for one normalized park. Every missing value shows its labelled fallback. */
export default function ParkDetails({ park }) {
  const headingRef = useRef(null)

  // Opening details (or moving to another park) puts focus on the heading.
  useEffect(() => {
    headingRef.current?.focus()
  }, [park.id])

  const address = display(park.address, NO_ADDRESS)
  const about = display(park.description, NO_DESCRIPTION)

  return (
    <article>
      <Link
        to="/"
        className="focus-ring inline-flex min-h-11 min-w-11 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface px-3 font-semibold text-forest hover:border-forest"
      >
        <svg
          aria-hidden="true"
          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          className="size-5"
          fill="none"
          stroke="currentColor"
          strokeWidth="2"
        >
          <path d="m15 6-6 6 6 6" />
        </svg>
        All parks
      </Link>

      <div className="mt-4">
        <PhotoArea key={park.id} park={park} />
      </div>

      <h1
        ref={headingRef}
        tabIndex={-1}
        className="mt-4 text-[28px] leading-tight font-extrabold"
      >
        {park.name}
      </h1>
      <p className="mt-1 text-muted">{address.text}</p>

      {park.hasCoords && (
        <p className="mt-2">
          <a
            href={osmUrl(park.lat, park.lng)}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="focus-ring inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded font-semibold text-forest underline"
          >
            Open in OpenStreetMap{' '}
            <span className="sr-only">(opens in new tab)</span>
          </a>
        </p>
      )}

      <dl className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
        <FactTile label="Hours" value={display(park.hours, NOT_LISTED)} />
        <FactTile label="Size" value={display(formatAcres(park.acreage), NOT_LISTED)} />
        <FactTile label="Rating" value={display(formatRating(park.rating), NO_RATING)} />
      </dl>

      <section aria-labelledby="park-about" className="mt-5">
        <h2 id="park-about" className="text-lg font-bold">
          About
        </h2>
        <p className={`mt-1 ${about.missing ? 'italic text-muted' : ''}`}>{about.text}</p>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="park-amenities" className="mt-5">
        <h2 id="park-amenities" className="text-lg font-bold">
          Amenities
        </h2>
        {park.amenities.length > 0 ? (
          <ul aria-label="Amenities" className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {park.amenities.map((token, index) => (
              <li
                key={`${index}-${token}`}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-tag-bg px-3 py-1 text-tag-fg"
              >
                <svg
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  className="size-4"
                  fill="none"
                  stroke="currentColor"
                  strokeWidth="2.5"
                >
                  <path d="m5 12 5 5 9-10" />
                </svg>
                {amenityLabel(token)}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        ) : (
          <p className="mt-1 italic text-muted">{NO_AMENITIES}</p>
        )}
      </section>
    </article>
  )
}

On desktop (≥ 768px), show a green header, a 400px independently scrolling sidebar and a map region filling the rest. On phone, show one column with a List / Map toggle (List default), and details full screen with an \"All parks\" back link in the green header. Then align tokens, type, radius and spacing with the design and close the accessibility checklist.\n\n## Boundaries & Constraints\n\n**Always:** `<header>`, `<main>`, `<aside aria-label=\"Parks\">` and `<section aria-label=\"Map of parks\">` stay; exactly one `<h1>` per view, including the loading and error states; every interactive element uses `focus-ring` and is ≥ 44×44px; no clickable `<div>`s; the toggle is two `<button type=\"button\">`s with `aria-pressed`; keep the existing focus-to-heading and focus-return-to-card behaviour and the `lastParkId` mechanism; no horizontal scroll at 375px.\n\n**Never:** Leaflet, markers, marker preview card or marker highlight (1.4); search, filters or geolocation; editing `parks.sample.json`, `src/data/parks.js` or the normalizer; adding a component library or a `matchMedia` JS breakpoint (layout switches with Tailwind `md:` classes only).\n\n**Decisions (agent-made, user-invisible or design-permitted):** the header uses an `aria-hidden` tree icon, app name as a `<p>` (the view owns the `<h1>`), and the subtitle \"Parks in the New York City area\" with the count right-aligned on desktop. On phone it reads \"12 parks · New York City area\". On phone details, the header shows only the \"‹ All parks\" link and the sidebar's bordered back link is `md:` only. Loading/error states sit under an \"All parks\" `<h1>`. `document.title` is \"Find a Park\" on the list and \"{Park name} · Find a Park\" on details. The toggle state lives in `App` and is ignored at desktop width (both panes always show). Choosing a park from the list on phone keeps the toggle at List.\n\n## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix\n\n| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Output / Behavior | Error Handling |\n|----------|--------------|---------------------------|----------------|\n| Phone list default | `/`, < 768px | toggle shows; List `aria-pressed=\"true\"`, Map `\"false\"`; list visible, map region hidden | N/A |\n| Phone Map tab | click Map | Map pressed; list pane gets `hidden`, map region shown | N/A |\n| Phone details | `/parks/:id`, < 768px | toggle and map hidden; header shows \"All parks\" link; one `<h1>` (park name) | N/A |\n| Desktop list | `/`, ≥ 768px | header with name, subtitle, \"12 parks\"; 400px aside scrolls; map fills rest; no toggle | N/A |\n| Desktop details | `/parks/:id`, ≥ 768px | sidebar shows details with bordered \"All parks\" button; map still visible | N/A |\n| Loading / error | fetch pending / rejects | \"All parks\" `<h1>` + status card; retry works | error card with Retry |\n| Not found | `/parks/nope` | one `<h1>` \"Park not found\" + way back | N/A |\n\n</frozen-after-approval>\n\n## Open Questions\n\n1. **Story 1.4 (map) is still `backlog`, but this story assumes the map exists.** The desktop map pane, the phone Map tab and the map's own controls (zoom 44px, attribution) all depend on 1.4. I've created branch `story/1-5-responsive-and-accessibility-polish-with-design-applied` from `develop` and drafted the spec. I need two answers before I can move to approval.

Spec: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-1-5-responsive-and-accessibility-polish-with-design-applied.md`

**The spec in brief:**
- **Layout:**
  - **Desktop (≥ 768px):** a green header (tree icon, "Find a Park", "Parks in the New York City area", "12 parks" on the right), a 400px sidebar that scrolls on its own, and the map area filling the rest.
  - **Phone:** a List / Map toggle under the header, List by default. Details take the full screen, with "‹ All parks" in the green header.
- **Matching the design:** I pulled the exact sizes and colours from `design/source/*.dc.html` (header, toggle, cards, labels, gaps).
- **Accessibility:** one `<h1>` in every view (loading and error now get an "All parks" heading), the amber focus ring and 44px targets on everything you can click, and a page title per view ("Prospect Park · Find a Park").

**1. Story 1.4 (the map) hasn't been built yet, but this story assumes it exists.** The map pane, the Map tab and the map's own accessibility checks all depend on it.
- **A — Build 1.5 now with a placeholder where the map goes (recommended).** The layout, toggle and accessibility pass land now, and 1.4 later drops the real map into that space. The zoom-button and marker checks move to 1.4, and the desktop comparison with the design stays partial until then.
- **B — Stop and do 1.4 first.** Then 1.5 can also polish the real map. This branch would sit unused for now.
- **C — Build the basic map inside 1.5.** That puts two separate deliverables in one story, which goes against the one-goal-per-story rule.

**2. Spec length.** The spec is about 2,000 tokens, or about 1,800 once question 1 is answered and removed, against a 1,600 target. Longer specs make it more likely the implementation drifts from them.
- **Keep full spec (recommended).** The extra length is mostly exact design values. Splitting them out would separate the layout from the styling it needs.
- **Split.** Move the type and spacing tweaks to the cards and details into a deferred item, and keep the shell, toggle and accessibility work here.

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, Route, Routes, useLocation, useMatch, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router'
import ParkDetails from './components/ParkDetails.jsx'
import ParkList from './components/ParkList.jsx'
import ParkMap from './components/ParkMap.jsx'
import ParkPreview from './components/ParkPreview.jsx'
import { useIsDesktop } from './data/useIsDesktop.js'
import { useParks } from './data/useParks.js'

const cardClass = 'rounded-[10px] border border-line bg-surface p-4'
const linkClass = 'focus-ring rounded text-forest underline'

function NotFound() {
  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-lg font-bold">Park not found</h1>
      <p className="mt-2">
        <Link to="/" className={linkClass}>
          Back to all parks
        </Link>
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

function ParkDetailsRoute({ parks, onOpen }) {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const park = parks.find((p) => p.id === id)

  // Record the opened park so the list can return focus to its card.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (park) onOpen(park.id)
  }, [park, onOpen])

  if (!park) return <NotFound />

  return <ParkDetails park={park} />
}

function ParksPanel({ status, parks, retry, lastParkId, setLastParkId }) {
  const clearLastParkId = useCallback(() => setLastParkId(null), [setLastParkId])

  if (status === 'loading') {
    return (
      <p role="status" className={cardClass}>
        Loading parks…
      </p>
    )
  }

  if (status === 'error') {
    return (
      <div role="alert" className={cardClass}>
        <p>Couldn't load parks. Try again.</p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={retry}
          className="focus-ring mt-3 min-h-11 rounded-lg bg-forest px-4 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-forest-dark"
        >
          Retry
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route
        path="/"
        element={
          <ParkList parks={parks} focusParkId={lastParkId} onFocusUsed={clearLastParkId} />
        }
      />
      <Route
        path="/parks/:id"
        element={<ParkDetailsRoute parks={parks} onOpen={setLastParkId} />}
      />
      <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
    </Routes>
  )
}

export default function App() {
  const { status, parks, retry } = useParks()
  // Id of the last-opened park. Lives in App state (not location.state) so
  // focus can return to its card after browser Back, which carries no state.
  const [lastParkId, setLastParkId] = useState(null)

  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const location = useLocation()
  const isDesktop = useIsDesktop()
  const selectedId = useMatch('/parks/:id')?.params.id ?? null
  // Phone-only marker preview; cleared on any navigation and when the viewport becomes desktop.
  const [previewId, setPreviewId] = useState(null)
  // Marker button to refocus after the preview closes.
  const [focusMarkerId, setFocusMarkerId] = useState(null)
  const clearFocusMarkerId = useCallback(() => setFocusMarkerId(null), [])

  useEffect(() => {
    setPreviewId(null)
  }, [location.key])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isDesktop && previewId) {
      // Focus may be inside the preview: hand it back to the marker, as closing does.
      setFocusMarkerId(previewId)
      setPreviewId(null)
    }
  }, [isDesktop, previewId])

  const activePreviewId = isDesktop ? null : previewId
  const highlightedId = selectedId ?? activePreviewId
  const previewPark =
    status === 'ready' && activePreviewId
      ? (parks.find((park) => park.id === activePreviewId) ?? null)
      : null

  const activateMarker = useCallback(
    (id) => {
      if (isDesktop) {
        // Re-activating the park already open would push a duplicate history entry.
        if (id !== selectedId) navigate(`/parks/${id}`)
      } else setPreviewId(id)
    },
    [isDesktop, navigate, selectedId],
  )

  const closePreview = useCallback(() => {
    setFocusMarkerId(previewId)
    setPreviewId(null)
  }, [previewId])

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <header className="bg-forest px-5 py-4 text-white">
        <p className="text-xl font-extrabold">Find a Park</p>
        <p className="text-sm">New York City area</p>
        {status === 'ready' && <p className="text-sm">{parks.length} parks</p>}
      </header>
      <main className="flex flex-1 flex-col">
        <aside aria-label="Parks" className="p-5">
          <ParksPanel
            status={status}
            parks={parks}
            retry={retry}
            lastParkId={lastParkId}
            setLastParkId={setLastParkId}
          />
        </aside>
        <section aria-label="Map of parks" className="p-5">
          {status === 'ready' && (
            <div className="relative isolate h-[480px]">
              <ParkMap
                parks={parks}
                selectedId={highlightedId}
                isDesktop={isDesktop}
                onActivate={activateMarker}
                focusId={focusMarkerId}
                onFocused={clearFocusMarkerId}
              />
              {previewPark && (
                <ParkPreview
                  key={previewPark.id}
                  park={previewPark}
                  total={parks.length}
                  onClose={closePreview}
                />
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </main>
    </div>
  )
}
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

const QUERY = '(min-width: 768px)'

function getMatch() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof window.matchMedia !== 'function') return true
  return window.matchMedia(QUERY).matches
}

/** True at viewport widths ≥ 768px. Without `matchMedia` (e.g. jsdom), assumes desktop. */
export function useIsDesktop() {
  const [isDesktop, setIsDesktop] = useState(getMatch)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof window.matchMedia !== 'function') return undefined
    const mql = window.matchMedia(QUERY)
    const onChange = () => setIsDesktop(mql.matches)
    onChange()
    mql.addEventListener('change', onChange)
    return () => mql.removeEventListener('change', onChange)
  }, [])

  return isDesktop
}
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react'
import L from 'leaflet'
import { MapContainer, Marker, TileLayer } from 'react-leaflet'
import 'leaflet/dist/leaflet.css'

// No-coordinates fallback: a fixed view over New York City.
const NYC_CENTER = [40.7128, -74.006]
const NYC_ZOOM = 11

const TILE_URL = 'https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png'
const TILE_ATTRIBUTION =
  '&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a> contributors'

// Full literal class strings so Tailwind's scanner picks them up.
const BUTTON_CLASS =
  'focus-ring flex size-11 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center rounded-full border-[3px] border-white bg-forest text-base font-bold text-white shadow-md'
const SELECTED_BUTTON_CLASS =
  'focus-ring flex size-14 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center rounded-full border-[3px] border-white bg-amber text-lg font-bold text-white shadow-lg'
const SIZE = 44
const SELECTED_SIZE = 56

function escapeHtml(value) {
  return String(value ?? '')
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;')
}

function markerIcon(park, selected) {
  const size = selected ? SELECTED_SIZE : SIZE
  const name = escapeHtml(`${park.number}. ${park.name}`)
  return L.divIcon({
    className: 'park-marker',
    iconSize: [size, size],
    iconAnchor: [size / 2, size / 2],
    html:
      `<button type="button" data-park-id="${escapeHtml(park.id)}" class="${
        selected ? SELECTED_BUTTON_CLASS : BUTTON_CLASS
      }">` +
      `<span aria-hidden="true">${escapeHtml(park.number)}</span>` +
      `<span class="sr-only">${name}</span>` +
      `</button>`,
  })
}

/**
 * OpenStreetMap map of the parks that have coordinates. Each marker is a single focusable
 * `<button>` named "N. Park name"; the highlighted park's marker is amber and larger.
 * `isDesktop` is part of the props contract but the map renders the same at every width; the
 * caller decides what activation does. `focusId` asks the map to put focus on that park's marker button.
 */
export default function ParkMap({ parks, selectedId, onActivate, focusId, onFocused }) {
  const wrapperRef = useRef(null)
  const mapped = useMemo(() => parks.filter((park) => park.hasCoords), [parks])

  // Stable icons per park and state, so unrelated renders never replace a marker's DOM.
  const icons = useMemo(() => {
    const cache = new Map()
    for (const park of mapped) {
      cache.set(park.id, { normal: markerIcon(park, false), selected: markerIcon(park, true) })
    }
    return cache
  }, [mapped])

  const bounds = useMemo(
    () => (mapped.length > 0 ? L.latLngBounds(mapped.map((p) => [p.lat, p.lng])) : null),
    [mapped],
  )

  // Runs after the markers' own effects, so it targets the current icon element.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!focusId) return
    const button = wrapperRef.current?.querySelector(
      `button[data-park-id="${CSS.escape(focusId)}"]`,
    )
    button?.focus()
    onFocused?.()
  }, [focusId, selectedId, onFocused])

  const viewProps = bounds
    ? { bounds, boundsOptions: { padding: [32, 32], maxZoom: 15 } }
    : { center: NYC_CENTER, zoom: NYC_ZOOM }

  return (
    <div ref={wrapperRef} className="h-full w-full">
      <MapContainer {...viewProps} className="h-full w-full rounded-[10px]">
        <TileLayer url={TILE_URL} attribution={TILE_ATTRIBUTION} />
        {mapped.map((park) => {
          const selected = park.id === selectedId
          const icon = icons.get(park.id)
          return (
            <Marker
              key={park.id}
              position={[park.lat, park.lng]}
              icon={selected ? icon.selected : icon.normal}
              keyboard={false}
              zIndexOffset={selected ? 1000 : 0}
              eventHandlers={{ click: () => onActivate?.(park.id) }}
            />
          )
        })}
      </MapContainer>
    </div>
  )
}
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import { display } from '../data/format.js'

const NO_ADDRESS = 'Address not listed'
const NO_HOURS = 'Hours not listed'

/** Phone-only preview card for a tapped marker. Focus moves to the card when it opens. */
export default function ParkPreview({ park, total, onClose }) {
  const cardRef = useRef(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    cardRef.current?.focus()
  }, [park.id])

  const address = display(park.address, NO_ADDRESS)
  const hours = display(park.hours, NO_HOURS)
  const headingId = `park-preview-${park.id}`

  return (
    <section
      ref={cardRef}
      tabIndex={-1}
      aria-labelledby={headingId}
      onKeyDown={(event) => {
        if (event.key === 'Escape') onClose()
      }}
      className="focus-ring absolute inset-x-3 bottom-8 z-[1001] rounded-2xl bg-surface p-4 shadow-lg"
    >
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-amber">
            Park {park.number} of {total}
          </p>
          <h2 id={headingId} className="text-xl font-extrabold">
            {park.name}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-muted">{address.text}</p>
          <p className="text-muted">
            {hours.missing ? (
              hours.text
            ) : (
              <>
                <span className="sr-only">Hours: </span>
                {hours.text}
              </>
            )}
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="Close park preview"
          onClick={onClose}
          className="focus-ring flex size-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-missing-bg text-ink"
        >
          <svg
            aria-hidden="true"
            viewBox="0 0 24 24"
            className="size-5"
            fill="none"
            stroke="currentColor"
            strokeWidth="2"
          >
            <path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6 6 18" />
          </svg>
        </button>
      </div>
      <Link
        to={`/parks/${park.id}`}
        className="focus-ring mt-3 flex min-h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-forest px-4 font-bold text-white hover:bg-forest-dark"
      >
        View park details
        <svg
          aria-hidden="true"
          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          className="size-5"
          fill="none"
          stroke="currentColor"
          strokeWidth="2"
        >
          <path d="m9 6 6 6-6 6" />
        </svg>
      </Link>
    </section>
  )
}
<frozen-after-approval reason="human-owned intent — do not modify unless human renegotiates">

## Intent

**Problem:** The "Map of parks" region is a "Map coming soon." placeholder. Residents cannot see where the parks are or pick one by location.

**Approach:** Render a react-leaflet map with OpenStreetMap tiles, fitted to the parks that have coordinates. Each such park gets a numbered, focusable marker that uses the loader's `number`. The park on `/parks/:id` has an amber, larger marker. At ≥ 768px, activating a marker navigates to `/parks/:id`. At < 768px it opens a preview card instead.

## Boundaries & Constraints

**Always:** use loader `number`/order unchanged; `hasCoords` decides which parks get markers; visible OSM attribution; one Tab stop per marker, a real `<button>`, name "N. Park name", `focus-ring`; touch targets ≥ 44px; preview copy shows a missing address as "Address not listed" and missing hours as "Hours not listed"; no API keys.

**Never:** geolocation (`navigator.geolocation`, `locate()`); a desktop two-column sidebar, a phone List/Map toggle, or any layout restyling (Story 1.5); details content (Story 1.3); panning the map on selection; editing `parks.sample.json`.

## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix

| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Output / Behavior | Error Handling |
|----------|--------------|---------------------------|----------------|
| Happy path | 12 sample parks at `/` | 12 marker buttons named "1. …" to "12. …", none amber | N/A |
| Selected | `/parks/prospect-park` | Prospect Park's marker amber and larger; all others forest | N/A |
| No coords | a park with `hasCoords: false` | no marker; still in the list | N/A |
| Desktop activate | ≥ 768px, click or Enter on a marker | URL `/parks/:id`, park heading in aside | N/A |
| Phone tap | < 768px, tap marker 8 | preview: "Park 8 of 12", name, address, hours, "View park details", "Close park preview"; marker 8 amber; URL unchanged | N/A |
| Preview missing address | < 768px, Highland Dog Park | "Address not listed" (muted) | N/A |
| Preview → details | press "View park details" | navigates to `/parks/:id` | N/A |
| Preview close | press "Close park preview" | preview gone; focus back on that marker | N/A |
| Not loaded | loading or error | map region shows no map | N/A |

</frozen-after-approval>
## Code Map

- `src/App.jsx` -- shell; the `<section aria-label="Map of parks">` placeholder is replaced. The map renders outside `<Routes>`, so App reads the selected id with `useMatch('/parks/:id')`. Keep `ParksPanel`, `ParkDetailsRoute`, `lastParkId`, the landmarks and the loading/error states as they are.
- `src/data/parks.js` -- park fields used: `id`, `name`, `number`, `address`, `hours`, `lat`, `lng`, `hasCoords`. Do not change it.
- `src/index.css` -- tokens `forest`, `amber`, `surface`, `muted`, `line`, and the `focus-ring` utility; Tailwind classes inside divIcon HTML strings are picked up only if the literal class names appear in a scanned source file.
- `src/App.test.jsx` -- `renderAt`, `okResponse`, and `TestNav`; extend these. jsdom has no `matchMedia`, so stub it per test.
- `design/screens/02-desktop-park-selected.png`, `05-phone-map-selected.png` -- marker and preview visuals: white-ringed forest number circles (selected: amber, ~1.3×); a white card at the bottom of the map with an amber "Park N of 12", forest full-width button and round close "×".
- Versions: `leaflet@^1.9.4`, `react-leaflet@^5.0.0` (peer React 19).

## Tasks & Acceptance
## Design Notes

**Marker as divIcon `<button>`:** a native button gives the name and Enter/Space for free, and Leaflet fires the marker `click` from the bubbling DOM click. `keyboard: false` keeps the wrapper div out of the Tab order. No default icon means no broken Vite icon paths.

**Layout before 1.5:** the map stays stacked under the aside at every width. Story 1.5 adds the sidebar and the List/Map toggle. Phone vs desktop behaviour is decided by viewport, not by layout, so 1.5 only restyles.

**Story 1.3 is merged:** `/parks/:id` renders `ParkDetailsRoute` → `ParkDetails`, which focuses its h1 on mount. A desktop marker activation therefore lands focus on the details heading.

## Verification

**Commands:**
- `npm test` -- expected: all tests pass.
- `npm run build` -- expected: succeeds with no errors.

**Manual checks (if no CLI):**
- `npm run dev`: tiles, attribution and 12 markers show; desktop click opens details with an amber marker; at 375px a tap shows the preview; no location prompt.

===== 2026-10-15T06:12:55.916000+00:00  [main]  assistant =====
I've pulled in the 1-4 commits (map, preview card, `useIsDesktop`) and rewritten the spec around them. The map question is gone. Two things still need your call before approval. Note that 1-4 is still marked `review` in sprint-status, so if its review leads to changes, this branch will need them too.

Spec: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-1-5-responsive-and-accessibility-polish-with-design-applied.md`

**What changed because of 1-4:**
- **Map sizing:** on phone the map sits in a hidden pane until you tap Map. Leaflet can't measure a hidden container, so the map would show grey tiles. The map will now resize itself whenever its container changes size.
- **Map edges:** the map goes edge to edge, matching screens 01 and 05, instead of a rounded box at a fixed 480px.
- **Zoom buttons:** Leaflet's zoom buttons grow to 44×44 and get the amber focus ring.
- **Phone vs desktop:** the layout switches with Tailwind's 768px breakpoint. 1-4's `useIsDesktop` hook keeps deciding only what tapping a marker does (open details vs show the preview card).
- **Preview card:** switching back to the List tab closes it.

**1. On phone, where should Back from details go if the user came from the map** (tap marker → preview → "View park details")?
- **A — Back to the Map tab, with focus on that park's marker (recommended).** The user ends up where they were. 1-4's existing marker refocus does this.
- **B — Always back to the List tab, with focus on the park's card.** This is simpler and matches the list behaviour from 1-2, but someone browsing the map gets moved to the list.

**2. Spec length.** It's about 1,900 tokens against a 1,600 target, and longer specs make it more likely the implementation drifts from them.
- **Keep full spec (recommended).** The extra is mostly exact sizes and colours from the design files, which are what this story is about.
- **Split.** Move the type and spacing tweaks to the cards and details into a deferred item, and keep the layout, toggle and accessibility work here.

===== 2026-10-15T06:14:14.738000+00:00  [main]  assistant =====
I've written in your answers: Back from the map returns to the Map tab with focus on the marker, and the full spec stays.

**On the 1M context question:** no, the 1,600 target has nothing to do with context size. A 1M window lets the model hold far more, but the target is about focus, not capacity. Each extra requirement is one more thing for the implementing agent to weigh, and in long specs the details in the middle are the ones most likely to get dropped. The bigger window doesn't change that much. At about 1,900 tokens, with most of the extra being exact design values, the risk here is low. It mainly shows up as a missed spacing value, which review should catch.

**The spec:** `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-1-5-responsive-and-accessibility-polish-with-design-applied.md`
- **Desktop (≥ 768px):** green header with the park count, a 400px sidebar that scrolls on its own, and an edge-to-edge map.
- **Phone:**
  - List / Map toggle under the header (`aria-pressed`), List by default.
  - Details take the full screen, with "‹ All parks" in the green header.
  - Back returns to whichever tab the park was opened from, with focus on the marker or the card.
- **Map:** it resizes itself when its pane appears or the window changes size, and Leaflet's zoom buttons become 44×44 with the amber focus ring.
- **Accessibility:** one `<h1>` in every view (loading and error included), amber focus ring and 44px targets throughout, and a page title per view.
- **Design match:** exact header, toggle, card and label sizes taken from `design/source/*.dc.html`.
- **Tests:** the toggle, one `<h1>` per view, focus return on both tabs, page titles, the preview closing when you switch to List, and the map resize.

14	
15	## Intent
16	
17	**Problem:** The shell stacks header, list and a fixed 480px map in one column at every width. There is no desktop sidebar/map split, no phone List/Map toggle, no phone details header, and sizes and spacing drift from `design/README.md` (CAP-4 full pass).
18	
19	**Approach:** Restyle the shell to the designed layouts. On desktop (≥ 768px), show a green header, a 400px independently scrolling sidebar and an edge-to-edge map filling the rest. On phone, show one column with a List / Map toggle (List default), and details full screen with "‹ All parks" in the green header. Then align sizes with the design and close the accessibility checklist, including Leaflet's own controls.
20	
21	## Boundaries & Constraints
22	
23	**Always:** keep the landmarks `<header>`, `<main>`, `<aside aria-label="Parks">` and `<section aria-label="Map of parks">`; exactly one `<h1>` per view, including loading/error; every interactive element (Leaflet zoom buttons included) shows the amber focus ring and is ≥ 44×44px; the toggle is two `<button type="button">`s with `aria-pressed`; keep 1.2–1.4 behaviour (heading focus, card focus return, marker activation by `useIsDesktop`, preview, marker refocus); no horizontal scroll at 375px; OSM attribution stays visible.
24	
25	**Never:** new map features (panning on select, clustering); search, filters or geolocation; editing `parks.sample.json` or `src/data/parks.js`; a component library. Layout switches with Tailwind `md:` classes. `useIsDesktop` keeps deciding behaviour only.
26	
27	**Decisions (agent-made, user-invisible or design-permitted):**
28	- The header has an `aria-hidden` tree icon and the app name as a `<p>`. On desktop it reads "Parks in the New York City area" with "12 parks" on the right; on phone, "12 parks · New York City area".
29	- On phone details the header shows only the "‹ All parks" link, and the sidebar's bordered back link is desktop-only.
30	- Loading/error sit under an "All parks" `<h1>`.
31	- `document.title` is "Find a Park" on the list and "{name} · Find a Park" on details.
32	- The toggle state lives in `App` and is ignored at desktop width.
33	- Switching to List closes any open preview.
34	
35	**Decisions (human):** On phone, Back from details opened via the map (marker → preview → "View park details") returns to the Map tab with focus on that park's marker. Back from details opened via the list returns to the List tab with focus on the card. The tab the details were opened from is remembered in `App` state, so browser Back works too.
36	
37	## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix
38	
39	| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Output / Behavior | Error Handling |
40	|----------|--------------|---------------------------|----------------|
41	| Phone list default | `/`, < 768px | List `aria-pressed="true"`, Map `"false"`; map pane hidden | N/A |
42	| Phone Map tab | press Map | Map pressed; list pane hidden; map pane shown and resized to fill it (tiles not grey) | N/A |
43	| Phone details | `/parks/:id`, < 768px | no toggle, no map; header "All parks" link; one `<h1>` | N/A |
44	| Desktop | ≥ 768px, any route | header with count; 400px aside scrolls alone; map fills rest; no toggle | N/A |
45	| Resize | phone Map tab → desktop → phone | both panes on desktop; map re-sizes; no stale preview | N/A |
46	| Loading / error | fetch pending / rejects | "All parks" `<h1>` + status card | Retry works |
47	
48	</frozen-after-approval>
